# WidgetRuntime's WPT expectations (M1b plan, Task 17). Written by `WidgetRuntimeWPTTests --update`; edit by
# hand only to lower a result on purpose, then update with --allow-regressions. One record per line, three
# tab-separated fields: <test> <subtest> <status>.
# <test>     the page's source path under third-party/wpt, plus its variant ("dom/nodes/Node-cloneNode.html",
#            "dom/events/Event-constructors.any.js", "dom/x.window.js?1-10").
# <subtest>  the subtest's name, with \ tab newline and carriage return written \\ \t \n \r and a leading # as
#            \#; a repeated name gets " (2)", " (3)", ...; or #harness for the file's harness status.
# <status>   subtests: PASS FAIL TIMEOUT NOTRUN PRECONDITION_FAILED. #harness: OK ERROR TIMEOUT
#            PRECONDITION_FAILED, or the runner's STOPPED (the runtime latched a stop) and NOCOMPLETE (no
#            completion before the runner's virtual-time limit).
# Records sort by <test>, then #harness first, then <subtest>, in byte order.
